- **z-accelerator**: Triggers on "accelerate my idea", "z-accelerator", "take this idea to market", "build me a launch package", "idea to market", "accelerate this", "market-ready package", "refine my idea", "run this through the pipeline", "prepare for market", "get this ready to launch". - **z-agent**: Dispatch parallel Agent calls for fan-out work — search/locate, mechanical edits, prose drafts, bounded multi-file, architecture. Triggers on "fan out", "in parallel", "across N files/repos", "use subagents", "split this", "agent team", "parallel scan", "z-agent", or any request that smells like N independent units of work. Respects the concurrency-lift cap (5 on laptop, higher on GPU), batches 3-5 at a time, waits between batches, and refuses wider fan-out unless explicitly asked. ([sabiridwan/my-claude-config](https://github.com/sabiridwan/my-claude-config/tree/HEAD/skills/z-agent))
- **z-agent-ticket-creation**: SOP for executor agents to create agent tickets when hitting blockers they cannot resolve independently. Invoke when a blocker is identified during executor cycles, loops, or scheduled tasks. ([numbersprotocol/yijing-divination](https://github.com/numbersprotocol/yijing-divination/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/z-agent-ticket-creation))

Read-only — never edits the target. ([zeke13dev/z-harness](https://github.com/zeke13dev/z-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/z-audit))
- **z-audit-plan**: Contract-level audit of a plan's INTENT, grounding assumptions, approach choices, and initial BFS task layer before execution. Low-risk plans receive a skip_recommended verdict and route directly to /z-execute. ([zeke13dev/z-harness](https://github.com/zeke13dev/z-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/z-audit-plan))

Emits PLAN_STYLE_AUDIT.md with BLOCKER/MAJOR/MINOR findings suitable for /z-amend. ([zeke13dev/z-harness](https://github.com/zeke13dev/z-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/z-audit-plan-style))
